### Page 1

Taiwan Semiconductor Manufacturing Co.

SEMICON Taiwan: proliferating AI boosting 
the manufacturing innovation

Maintain Rating: BUY | PO: 3,100 TWD | Price: 2,420 TWD

HPC computing drives holistic manufacturing innovation 
SEMICON Taiwan kicked off on August 31st with IC Forum and Silicon Photonics Summit, 
both featuring TSMC’s presentations. At the session in IC Forum, TSMC shared its view 
that the data center power capacity will accelerate from 5-6GW per year from ‘15-24 to 
30-40GW per year from ‘25-30, reaching 300GW installed base by ’30 as AI training 
scales toward multimodal (5x per annum LLM compute growth). In addition, it sees 
robust AI inference demand, with token consumption growing ~500x since ’22 led by 
agents and reasoning and further compounding by 24x to 120 quadrillion tokens by ‘30. 
TSMC has AI platform across logic (A13, A12, N2U). Si-technology for HBM scaling (from 
N12 for HBM4 to N3 for HBM5) and system integration (e.g. SoIC with 11 tape outs for 
GPU/CPU by 4Q26 and >14x reticle size CoWoS by ‘29) and compute fabric (e.g. CPO). 
Silicon photonics acceleration to enable faster I/O scaling 
TSMC believes the optical I/O is becoming prerequisite for AI/HPC system as the I/O gap 
is widening between the compute scaling (3x every 2 years) and I/O scaling (1.4x every 
two years). Its COUPE platform and a broad photonics device/PDK library facilitate its 
customer designs to enhance the connectivity through two complementary paths – 
“faster and narrowing” with higher per lane speed (200Gbps to 400Gbps) and lane count 
(128+ lanes) and “slower and wider” by increasing wavelengths from 1 to 16+ through 
WDM. To allow further production ramp and wider adoption of the technology platform, 
TSMC has lowered COUPE grating coupler insertion loss while improved edge coupler 
performance and sees wafer level testing is critical to reduce the wasted packaging cost. 
Stock at mid-cycle supported by robust AI demand pipeline 
We maintain our Buy rating with PO at NT$3,100 on 20x '27 P/E. The share valuation is 
undemanding at 16x '27 P/E on strong technology leadership and extending AI visibility.

Company Sector 
Semiconductors

Company Description 
TSMC was founded in 1987 and is listed in Taiwan 
(2330TT) and the US (TSM/NYS).TSMC is the largest and 
global leader in integrated circuit (IC) manufacturing. As a 
build-to-order foundry, it provides a wide range of value-
add activities: IC manufacturing, mask-making, IC design 
services, turnkey solutions, and process development. We 
attribute its success to its proven, winning business model, 
unparalleled scale advantage, optimized execution, and 
technology scope and depth. 5 shares = 1 ADR.

Investment Rationale 
The ongoing semi content growth in mobile, rise of artificial 
intelligence (AI), and proliferation of Internet of Things (IoT) 
should result in sustainable upside in aggregate computing 
power globally. TSMC, as the leading contract manufacturer 
of semiconductor chips, is in a good position to capitalize. 
We are structurally positive on TSMC and expect it to 
sustain over 15% growth with rising FCF from 2026 
onwards.

### Page 4

Price objective basis & risk 
Taiwan Semiconductor Manufacturing Co. (TSMWF / TSM) 
Our PO for TSMC is NT$3,100 per share (US$590 per ADR), based on 20x 2027E P/E. 
TSMC's valuation has traded in the range of 10-26x PE from 2017. 20x is in the mid to 
high end of the historical range, above the average P/E of 18x. We think a PE above the 
average is justified given TSMC's enhanced industry position and mid-40% 5-year CAGR 
in AI should underpin a rerating

Downside risks to our price objective are: (1) greater-than-expected slowdown in global 
smartphone/consumer electronics demand, (2) Intel's potential insourcing strategy and 
ambitions in foundry service, (3) execution risks on advanced nodes, and (4) higher than 
expected tariffs.

financial instruments) nor should the analyses or opinion(s) be considered a solicitation of any kind. Sales persons and fina ncial advisors affiliated with BofAS or any of its affiliates may not
solicit purchases of securities or financial instruments that are Restricted or Under Review and may only solicit securities under Extended Review in accordance with firm policies.

2357.TT 華碩 
 2H26 AI 動能續強

[財務資訊更新]

公司簡介 
華碩電腦成立於 1990 年，以生產個人電腦主機板起家，後來跨入筆記型電腦、無線
網通、及 3C 消費電子產品領域，以自有品牌「ASUS」銷售全球。2006 年以 ROG 
(Republic of Gamers) 品牌發展高階電競主機板及筆電等產品，樹立在電競市場的地
位，近年亦開始發展 AI 伺服器、AIoT 產品，搶攻 AI 商機。 
投資評等/目標價 重點摘要 
買進 1,130 
【 前 次 投 資 建 議 】區 間 操 作，840 
【大盤指數/股價】47183 / 979

➢ 2H26 AI 伺服器強勢成長  
華碩 8 月營收 949.6 億元 ，MoM+12.9%，YoY+51.2%， 公司預期3Q26 
PC 營收 QoQ+15%~20% ， YoY+20%~30% ， 零 組 件 營 收  
QoQ+5%~10%、YoY 持平，伺服器營收 QoQ+10%，YoY+150%。雖
然 PC 產業面臨缺料與關鍵零組件成本上升的挑戰，但記憶體的漲價帶
動 PC 售價上升，加上華碩高價值產品(如:高階電競、Copilot+PC 等)已
占營收 65%以上，此類產品的消費者價格敏感度較低，因此公司認為
3Q26 PC 業務價、量都優於 2Q26。 
華碩 AI 伺服器業務持續強勁 ， 客戶包含CSP、Neo cloud 以及主權 AI，
公司的伺服器營收當中，90%由 AI 貢獻(AI 中 HGX 約占營收 65%、
GB300 約占營收 35%)，預期伺服器 2026 全年營收 YoY+150%。投研
部估計華碩 3Q26 營收 2,773.6 億元，QoQ+7.6%，YoY+38.5%，毛利
率 16.3%，EPS 25.10 元。  
➢ IFA 2026 展出搭載 NVIDIA RTX Spark 的 ProArt PC 
華碩於 IFA 2026 展前發表會中 ， 已發表搭載 RTX Spark 的 ProArt P16、
ProArt P14、ProArt GR1X mini PC：1) ProArt P16 機身厚度 12.9 mm、
重量 1.77kg， 相較前一代薄14%、輕 9%， 為該系列歷來為最輕薄的16
吋版本；2) ProArt P14 機身厚度 13.9 mm、重量 1.48kg，搭載 90Wh
電池提供全天候續航；3) ProArt GR1X 尺寸 150×150×51 mm ，主打
機身僅掌心大小 ，同時兼具強大AI 運算效能 。 華碩表示，ProArt P16 與
ProArt P14 首波已預購一空，新品將於 4Q26 出貨。關於 AI PC 市場，
華碩之 AI PC 2Q26 已在消費型 NB 營收中達到 30%占比，公司認為未
來 2~3 年 AI PC、Edge AI 將引發產品升級循環 ， 對2H26 亦樂觀看待 。 
➢ 建議買進，目標價 1,130 元 
華碩 2H26 在 AI 伺服器與 AI PC 的帶動下展望樂觀，預期 2027 年 AI
伺服器能為公司帶來穩定的成長動能 。 華碩歷史PE 區間為 9X~18X，考
量公司營運展望正向 ， 投研部認為有利帶動其PE 回升 ， 買進目標價1,130
元(2027F PE 13X)。

2H26 AI 伺服器強勢成長 
華碩 8 月營收 949.6 億元，MoM+12.9%，YoY+51.2%，公司預期 3Q26 PC 營收
QoQ+15%~20%、YoY+20%~30%，零組件營收 QoQ+5%~10%、YoY 持平，伺服
器營收 QoQ+10%，YoY+150%。雖然 PC 產業面臨缺料與關鍵零組件成本上升的挑
戰 ， 但 記 憶 體 的 漲 價 帶 動PC 售 價 上 升 ， 加 上 華 碩 高 價 值 產 品( 如:高 階 電 競、
Copilot+PC 等)已占營收 65%以上， 此類產品的消費者價格敏感度較低， 因此公司認
為 3Q26 PC 業務價、量都優於 2Q26。 
華碩 AI 伺服器業務持續強勁，客戶包含 CSP、Neo cloud 以及主權 AI，公司的伺服
器營收當中，90%由 AI 貢獻(AI 中 HGX 約占營收 65%、GB300 約占營收 35%)，預
期伺服器 2026 全年營收 YoY+150%。投研部估計華碩 3Q26 營收 2,773.6 億元，
QoQ+7.6%，YoY+38.5%，毛利率 16.3%，EPS 25.10 元。 
因應 AI 伺服器龐大資金需求 ，董事會於 2026 年 8 月通過將發行上限達 15 億美元(約
新台幣 483 億元)的海外無擔保可轉換公司債(ECB)，並且計畫以研華(2395)持股發行
10~11 億美元的海外可交換公司債(EB)。

IFA 2026 展出搭載 NVIDIA RTX Spark 的 ProArt PC 
NVIDIA RTX Spark 是專門為 Windows AI PC 推出的超級晶片 (Superchip)，將
NVIDIA AI 與 RTX 繪圖效能結合在單一晶片中(Blackwell RTX GPU+20 核心 Crace 
CPU)，這也是 NVIDIA 首次跨足 Windows PC 處理器市場市場。該晶片主要搭載於
輕薄 NB 與高效能迷你桌上型電腦，目標客戶為創作者、AI 開發者、電競玩家。 
華碩於 IFA 2026 展前發表會中， 已發表搭載 RTX Spark 的 ProArt P16、ProArt P14、
ProArt GR1X mini PC ：1)ProArt P16 機身厚度 12.9 mm、重量 1.77kg，相較前一
代薄 14%、輕9%， 為該系列歷來為最輕薄的16 吋版本 ；2)ProArt P14 機身厚度 13.9 
mm、重量 1.48kg，搭載 90Wh 電池提供全天候續航；3)ProArt GR1X 尺寸 150×
150×51 mm，主打機身僅掌心大小，同時兼具強大 AI 運算效能。 
華碩表示，ProArt P16 與 ProArt P14 首波已預購一空，新品將於 4Q26 出貨。關於
AI PC 市場，華碩之 AI PC 2Q26 已在消費型 NB 營收中達到 30%占比，公司認為未
來 2~3 年 AI PC、Edge AI 將引發產品升級循環，對 2H26 亦樂觀看待。

股價相對大盤走勢

聯發科 (2454 TT)  Buy

輝達認購聯發科 ECB，將帶動聯發科短期評價，投資建議
給 Buy。  
投資建議 ：輝達 (NVDA US) 認購聯發科 ECB ，有助於短期評價
與長期營運，投資建議給 Buy 。  
輝達認購聯發科 ECB：輝達宣布斥資 35 億美元，認購聯發科的
ECB ，其餘 ECB 認購公司包含 Alphabet(GOOG US) 。  
聯發科將資金用於後續 AI 商機：聯發科此次募資資金將用於多
項用途，除了強化全球供應鏈外，也將持續加碼晶片設計、
ASIC 、互連 IP 以及先進封裝能力，藉此支援後續更大型的 AI
專案。  
雙方將有更多合作： 未來加強合作領域包含以下三點。一、 AI
基礎建設，聯發科將會基於 NVLink Fusion 生態系，協助客戶
開發基礎建設，並整合進 NVIDIA 的機櫃及 AI 工廠。二、邊緣
AI 運算，雙方會開發更多 RTX Spark 與 DGX Spark 的電腦晶
片。三、車用，雙方將持續開發具備 AI 功能的軟體定義汽車平
台。  
輝達投資聯發科，有助於聯發科 XPU 商機： 聯發科已與部分潛
在客戶展開接洽， NVIDIA 直接投資聯發科，可讓客戶更願意採
用聯發科 XPU 搭配 NVLink Fusion 的整體方案，也可讓雙方擴
大供應鏈與產品版圖。  
群益預估聯發科 2026 年 \2027 年稅後 EPS 60.57 元 \80.92 元：
群益預估聯發科 2026 年 \2027 年營收為 6,138.45 億元 \10,435.36
億元， YoY+3.00%\+70.00% ；稅後純益 971.44 億元 \1,297.92 億
元， YoY-7.76%\+33.61% ；稅後 EPS 60.57 元 \80.92 元。

### Page 2

個股報告  
                                        2026 年 9 月 1 日

2 群益投顧 
  ESG ：  
環境保護：聯發科技做為全球半導體產業技術領導者，在整體價值鏈中位於
最前端的晶片設計，後端的原物料採購、代工製造、封裝與測試等環節皆交
由供應商執行，形成專業分工與互相合作的夥伴關係。根據風險調查與碳足
跡盤查發現，主要對社會與環境產生衝擊影響的關鍵在於後端製造作業，故
如何與供應商共同合作，以降低社會與環境相關的潛在風險，為聯發科技發
展綠色營運的首要任務。  
社會責任：運用數位科技解決社會問題不是企業的專利，聯發科技相信每個
人都有潛力利用數位科技創造無限可能，聯發科發起數位社會創新競賽 「智
在家鄉」，鼓勵社會大眾為家鄉問題提供解方。  
公司治理：聯發科技董事會下設有審計委員會，強化公司治理獨立性，並設
立薪資報酬委員會，以健全公司薪酬制度為目標，持續強化企業永續發展。
除前述法定功能性委員會外，本公司亦設立法定以外之併購策略委員會，視
實際需求針對集團具體併購案件及整體併購策略評估分析。  
產品組合：  
2Q26 產品組合：手機晶片業務 41% 、智慧裝置平台業務 53% 、 PMIC 6% 。  
手機業務全年展望保守：  
2Q26 手機晶片業務營收 QoQ-14%\YoY-20% ，營收占比 41% ，衰退原因是
手機的 BOM 表的成本上漲，聯發科預計 2026 年全球手機出貨量 YoY 約 -
15% 。  
客戶重視旗艦級產品與價格敏感的入門級產品 ， 聯發科 3Q26 將推出 2nm 製
程的 SoC ，為下一代 AI 模型提供動力，聯發科與全球廠商合作，持續擴大
2027 年旗艦級手機的市占率。  
供應鏈成本上漲已經是產業現象，聯發科將對價格進行調整，以確保成本上
漲可以反映在產品定價中。 3Q26 手機業務展望季持平或季減中個位數百分
比，旗艦級 SoC 的量產可以抵消大部分其他手機業務的衰退。  
客戶重視旗艦級產品與價格敏感的入門級產品 ， 聯發科 3Q26 將推出 2nm 製
程的 SoC ，為下一代 AI 模型提供動力，聯發科與全球廠商合作，持續擴大
2027 年旗艦級手機的市占率。  
智慧邊緣平台業務展望正向：  
2Q26 智慧邊緣平台業務營收 QoQ+19\YoY+26% ，營收占比 53%。成長動能
包含在運算、車用的市佔率的提升，以及 TV SoC 的成長。多項連接、車用
項目走向量產，聯發科預計此業務將季增中高個位數營收。  
2Q26 PMIC 營收占比約 6%：  
2Q26 PMIC 業務營收 QoQ+11%\YoY+6% ，營收占比 6%。成長動能包含資
料中心的市占率提升，聯發科預計此業務將季持平。

### Page 3

個股報告  
                                        2026 年 9 月 1 日

3 群益投顧 
AI ASIC 業務展望更樂觀：  
聯發科第一個 AI 加速器 ASIC 將於 4Q26 開始量產，預計 2026 全年資料中
心將貢獻超過 20 億美元，並且在 2027 年倍數成長，聯發科預計 AI ASIC 在
2027 年的市場規模將達到 800 億美元，聯發科目標市佔率從 10~15% ，上修
至 15~20% 。第二個 AI 加速器 ASIC 將於 2028 年初量產，聯發科有信心在
2028 年的市占率將提升。  
聯發科認為每代的 ASIC 的毛利率相當，並不會更高， ASIC 的毛利率低於
目前公司平均，但新世代的 ASIC 對營收的貢獻貴更大。  
其他補充：  
聯發科 448G SerDes 的研發順利，採用 CPO 的解決方案。 448G SerDes 之
後，聯發科會在台積電 (2330 TT) 的 COUPE 平台上，繼續發展 CPO 的解決
方案。聯發科還有提供 3.5D 的平台，該平台提供 IP、封裝與設計流程，適
用於大型資料中心的設計。  
供應鏈成本上漲已經是產業現象，聯發科將對價格進行調整，以確保成本上
漲可以反映在產品定價中。聯發科目前還在投入新技術，絕對費用金額還會
再增加，但是由於營收規模上升，費用率會下降。

7 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

Company Sector 
Semiconductors

Company Description 
MediaTek is a fabless IC design company supplying to 
wireless communications and digital consumer markets. 
Before taking leadership in mobile SoC area, it had built 
solid ground in the TV SoC and optical disk drive markets. 
The firm invests heavily in R&D to seek better positioning 
in the 5G cycle, and has a complete offering including ASIC, 
Wi-Fi, PMIC etc. to address applications beyond 
smartphone, such as IoT and auto.

Investment Rationale 
We have Buy rating on MediaTek as we think MediaTek is 
ready and has the strength to brace for the upcoming 
proliferation of IoT devices and opportunities in growing 
markets like ASIC, ARM-based PC, auto, and etc., and we 
believe its solid balance sheet and cashflow could provide 
downside support to the share price. Meanwhile, we expect 
5G smartphone penetration to be a multi-year theme for 
the company, with its better competitive landscape in 
duopoly 5G era and an improved margin profile.

Stock Data  
Price to Book Value 14.4x

### Page 3

Price objective basis & risk 
MediaTek (MDTKF) 
Our PO of NT$5,230 is based on 23x 2H27-1H28 P/E. as we are constructive on its 
broadening ASIC pipeline to drive earnings upgrade cycle and re-rating as it locks in 
more supply.

Upside risks to our PO are: (1) better than expected smartphone demand, especially in 
China, (2) a better pricing in mobile communication segment thanks to alleviated 
competition and rising exposure to high-end/flagship market, and (3) an even more 
superior investment return on non-op side to uplift its net cash position. (4) Unexpected 
collaboration with global technology leaders leads to higher than expected 
margins/profits

Downside risks to our PO are: (1) weaker-than-expected end-market situation in 
consumer-related smartphone/TV space, (2) macro challenges including inflation/policy-
related pressures, (3) rising competition from existing players and/or newcomers in 
smartphone/TV SoC (system on chip) space, and (4) ASP/margin pressure caused by each 
or all of the above and/or rising foundry costs. (4) Bidding process for Meta Inference 
ASIC is slower than expected.

FUNDAMENTAL EQUITY OPINION KEY: Opinions include a Volatility Risk Rating, an Investment Rating and an Income Rating.  VOLATILITY RISK RATINGS, indicators 
of potential price fluctuation, are: A - Low, B - Medium and C - High. INVESTMENT RATINGS reflect the analyst’s assessment of both a stock’s absolute total return 
potential as well as its attractiveness for investment relative to other stocks within its Coverage Cluster (defined below). Our investment ratings are: 1 - Buy stocks 
are expected to have a total return of at least 10% and are the most attractive stocks in the coverage cluster; 2 - Neutral stocks are expected to remain flat or 
increase in value and are less attractive than Buy rated stocks and 3 - Underperform stocks are the least attractive stocks in a coverage cluster. An investment 
rating of 6 (No Rating) indicates that a stock is no longer trading on the basis of fundamentals. Analysts assign investment ratings considering, among other things, 
the 0-12 month total return expectation for a stock and the firm’s guidelines for ratings dispersions (shown in the table below). The current price objective for a 
stock should be referenced to better understand the total return expectation at any given time. The price objective reflects the analyst’s view of the potential price 
appreciation (depreciation).

Airtac
Valuation
We derive our target price of NT$1,750 for Airtac by using a P/E methodology, as we believe the margin and earnings growth
outlooks will be the key drivers of the stock. We apply a P/E of 27.5x for 2H26-1H27E, which we derive from the mid range of
prior upcycle valuation 25-35x during 2021 in view of a strong upcycle with solid FA demand and ongoing share gains.
Risks
Key downside risks that could impede the Airtac stock from reaching our target price include: 1) a softer-than-expected China

macro economy, resulting in downside to our sales forecasts; 2) intensifying competition leading to gross margin pressures;
and 3) slower-than-expected progress of its linear guide business, causing low utilization of capacity.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

ab 6 September 2026
Global Research
Largan Precision
Strong CPO ramp priced ahead of qualification
The re-rating has outpaced qualification progress; downgrade from Buy to Sell
Largan shares have risen 242% since April on optimism around its CPO FAU progress.
While we stay constructive on Largan's CPO prospects, we believe the market is pricing
in confidence ahead of still-early qualification progress, particularly given a crowded
competitive field. Largan only starts its sample line late Q3, integrating its Prism Micro

Lens Array and Fiber Array with AOET's V-groove and Corning's fiber. We expect  several
quarters to improve yields, gain integrator acceptance (Corning, Senko, AuthenX) and
convert its initial production-oriented FA program into volume while competing against
incumbent TFC and vertically integrated rivals incl. Coherent. We downgrade our rating
from Buy to Sell with CPO still at an early stage and the stock now 35x 2027 vs. LT 13-
18x range well above our sum-of-the-parts NT$5,000 PT based on 15x smartphone lens

+ 50x CPO base case for 40% share  (upper end of optical peers  at 14-72x).
Still a range of outcomes for CPO solution traction
Largan's approach pairs V-groove with PMLA to shape the optical light from the PIC into
the fiber at high accuracy with automated assembly. Feedback is positive on Largan's
precision and track record for volume production/yield improvement (now still low) and
1 FA design in an FAU. A risk is Largan targeting the Gen 3 optics should take until 2028

and also competes with incumbent TFC which is already designed-in for 2026-27 and
other FAU vendors like Coherent that can vertically integrate or use meta lens not
requiring Largan's initial V-groove approach. Largan's push toward a complete solution
may also conflict with integration partners.
Core smartphone lens tracking behind amid memory headwinds
We also see risk on near-term results with Q326 sales tracking light. Aug sales were NT
$5bn, +3% MoM, -16% YoY, taking QTD revenue to NT$10.1bn, 53% of UBSe/Street

Q326. Sept would need to grow +73% MoM to meet UBSe/Street +36-38% QoQ vs.
normal seasonal +8% MoM. We expect flattish builds at best for its flagship customer
(80% of sales) needing to raise price to pass on memory costs +US$200-300 YoY albeit
some support from high-end introducing variable-aperture lens, while foldable lens
specs remain similar. Factoring muted smartphone growth but healthy margins, we view
a mid-teens multiple appropriate for this business, in line with its historical range.

Stock prices in our upside case ahead of traction; risk-reward skews negative
We maintain basic '26E/27E EPS at NT$190/NT$214 and NT$5,000 SOTP PT based on 1)
15x smartphone lens (NT$3,270) + 2) 50x CPO base case EPS of NT$35 for NT$1,730
(40% share, 100k switches/7.2mn FAUs, US$150/FAU, US$1.1bn TAM), high-end of
peers due to its premium ~40-45% OpM. We view risk/reward skewed negative with
downside to NT$2,500 on limited CPO success and softer smartphones, base case NT

$5,000 for CPO traction, and upside 22% if the stock is bid to our upside case NT$9,000
based on top-end 70x P/E on CPO earnings at 60% share of a higher US$1.58bn TAM.

Largan Precision 6 September 2026 ab 2
Largan Precision UBS Research
Thesis Map     UBS                    Research     THESIS MAP a guide to our thinking and what´s where in this report
Q: Could Largan's CPO products contribute meaningful revenue in a few years?
We stay constructive but the company still has hurdles to clear in a crowded space. Its scale and track
record in high-end smartphone lens gives it an opportunity supplying key components of the CPO

FAU assembly, traditional strength in precision lens micro assemblies, alignment accuracy, in-house
tooling and automation that could give it a differentiated solution. Our feedback at Semicon Taiwan
suggests the company is still early in qualification, with its sample production line for customer
evaluation readied in late September and still requiring work with a system integrator and
qualification with the fabless customer and ODMs.  We still see a year to move from sampling to

design-in but if successful timed to meet expected market acceleration in 2028-29.
Q: Will there be further technology upgrades driving the lens cycle in the coming years?
We expect spec upgrades for Largan's core lens business to come in H226 from variable aperture
shipments for the iPhone 18 (which Genius does not currently participate though may enter over
time, as seen with periscope lenses). Key offset though is weaker sales with Q3 tracking light and

suggesting a softer build due to memory headwinds. Further upside could come from broader
adoption of 48MP modules in future iPhone models. Looking longer term, Apple is reportedly
exploring 200MP periscope lens and adjacent opportunities to penetrate physical AI categories with
precision lens including robotics, autonomous systems and AR/VR devices.
We downgrade our rating on Largan from Buy to Sell as we view the market expectations as now
pricing in a bullish scenario for strong market traction with the company still at an early stage of

qualification, still needing to improve its process and yields, and competing in a crowded space also
against an existing incumbent. We expect several quarters to improve current low yields, gain
acceptance from solution integrators (Corning, Senko, AuthenX) and secure design-ins by the AI GPU
fabless company and its ODM partners against incumbent TFC and other rivals. We downgrade
Largan from Buy to Sell with CPO still at an early stage and the stock now 35x 2027 vs. LT 13-18x

range and well above our sum-of-the-parts NT$5,000 price target based on 15x smartphone lens +
50x CPO base case for40% share  (upper-end of optical peer range of 14-72x).
Largan has demonstrated its samples with partners at Computex and Semicon Taiwan and cited its
goals to ramp CPO as its next opportunity. The company is still early in qualifications with a sample
automated line in the coming months followed by design-in engagement with system integrators,

ODMs and the fabless customer to target design-in on 3rd gen CPO solutions in 2028. For
smartphone lens, its top customer had a strong iPhone 17 cycle although acknowledged it would
have to raise price to pass on higher memory costs which could dampen growth. Sales QTD are
tracking slightly light as Largan also competes with 2nd source Genius. Our supply chain checks and
management commentary points to introduction of higher value variable aperture lens in H226.

Largan is currently trading above the high end of its historical range at 39x/35x our 2026E/27E EPS vs.
a long-term range of 13-18x forward consensus earning, pricing in success penetrating the CPO
component market ahead of the earnings stream which would only start late in 2027 into 2028. We
note optical component peers trade at 14-72x with an average of ~38x 2027E P/E (Genius at 25x
2027 P/E). We maintain our SOTP valuation with our price target at NT$5000 based on 1) 15x

smartphone lens (NT$3,270) + 2) 50x CPO base case EPS of NT$35 for NT$1,730 (40% share, 100k
switches/7.2mn FAUs, US$150/FAU, US$1.1bn TAM), high-end of peers due to its premium ~40-
45% OpM. We view the stock at NT$7,400 now pricing in CPO traction on a 50x multiple of our
upside NT$76 case based on 60% share of an upside US$1.6bn opportunity.

### Page 3

Largan Precision 6 September 2026 ab 3
Largan Precision UBS Research
commentary
Largan shares have risen 242% since 1 April on optimism around its CPO fiber array unit
(FAU) progress. While we stay constructive post our feedback at Semicon Taiwan on
Largan's ability to develop CPO solutions, we now believe the market is pricing in a level
of confidence that has run well ahead of qualification progress which is still early and
competing in a crowded field. Largan will only start its sample line in late Q3 integrating

its Prism Micro Lens Array and Fiber Array, AOET's V-groove and Corning's glass fiber.
From there, we expect several quarters to improve current low yields, gain acceptance
from solution integrators (Corning, Senko, AuthenX) and secure design-ins by the AI
GPU fabless company and its ODM partners against incumbent TFC and rivals including
Coherent with own lens/FAU. The company did note on its Q226 call that it had secured
one mass production commitment for its fiber array to an FAU maker that could ramp by

H227 although early to conclude the magnitude of this engagement or end customer
design-in, with the other customers still at proof-of-concept phase.
We downgrade our rating on Largan from Buy to Sell with CPO still at an early stage and
the stock now 35x 2027 vs. LT 13-18x range and well above our sum-of-the-parts NT
$5,000 price target based on 15x smartphone lens + 50x CPO base case for 40% share
(upper-end of optical component peers trading at a  range of 14-72x).

Source: UBS estimates
Still a range of outcomes for CPO solution traction
Largan's approach pairs V-groove with PMLA to shape the optical light from the PIC into
the fiber at high accuracy with automated assembly. Feedback is positive on Largan's
precision and track record for volume production/yield improvement (now still low). A
risk is Largan targeting the Gen 3 optics should take until 2028 and also competes with

incumbent TFC which is already designed-in for 2026-27 and other FAU vendors like
Coherent that can vertically integrate or use meta lens not requiring Largan's initial V-
groove approach. Largan's business model may also conflict with integration partners as
it also attempts to build toward a complete solution.
We estimate Largan's Total Addressable Market in a downside case is US$270mn if it
only supplies sub-components into a market that continues to push out CPO due to

reliability concerns relative to pluggables and NPO/XPO options, US$1.08bn in a base
case with 100k switches and 72 FAUs per switch at US$150 ASP , and US$1.6bn in an
upside case with 125k switches, 72 FAUs per switch and US$175 ASP .  For Largan, we
estimate downside case 10% share yields NT$810mn sales and NT$2.20 EPS, base case
40% share, NT$13.0bn sales and NT$35 EPS, and upside case 60% share, NT$28.4bn
sales and NT$76 EPS.
Although we are optimistic on the market opportunity scaling up and Largan's

competencies and progress developing these solutions, Largan's traction is not yet
certain and it will compete in a crowded landscape. We believe Himax is already
supplying wafer level optics for the collimator function with FOCI (3363.TWO) and TFC
(300394.SZ) already has demonstrated capability and design-in on the FAU and lens
assembly. Others that could vertically integrate include Coherent, Senko, Corning and
Sumitomo, and we also note Genius sending samples and Sunny Optical's intention to

develop for this market.

Our feedback at Semicon Taiwan indicates Coherent can make the FAU and lens by itself
through capabilities merging with II-VI and demonstrated all in-house components at
the booth except for the metal transceiver casing.

Smartphone lens business tracking behind, should have a mild 2026-27
Largan reported August sales at NT$5bn,   is below seasonal 63-67% at only 53% of
Q326 UBSe/Street +36-38% QoQ and down 16% YoY, suggesting muted momentum
into its flagship customer (~80% of sales). July EPS was NT$13.94, 27% of UBSe Q3 NT
$51.52 suggesting in-line GM/OpM at ~50%/39% but the lighter sales are more of a

concern.
UBS expects flattish builds at best and a mild spec upgrade with one model variable
aperture but foldable at similar lens spec and higher memory cost (+US$200-300 YoY).
We still see mild growth and mid-teens multiple acceptable for the smartphone
business, similar to its long-term average.

Largn (3008.TW) trading at 7,400 at closed Sept 4.

UPSIDE (NT$9,000): Our upside valuation of NT$3,655 is based on  its average P/E of 16x
off our 2027 upside case EPS of NT$235. We factor in faster than expected
CPO progress to deliver upside EPS of NT$76 and 70x P/E at the high end of optical peers
trading at 14-72x 2027E P/E to an implied valuation of NT$5,345, and  total share price at
NT$9,000.
BASE (NT$5,000):  Our price target for its smartphone business is NT$3,270, with a 15x
2027 P/E applied on its base case EPS of NT$214 slightly below the long-term average of

its historical P/E band to reflect the macro uncertainty. Paired with our base scenario of its
CPO business at NT$35 EPS and 50x P/E, we arrive at an implied valuation of NT$1,730,
and Largan's aggregate PT  at NT$5,000.
DOWNSIDE (NT$2,500):   Downside valuation at NT$2,392, based on low-end of its
range 13x 2027 P/E. We take into consideration slower adoption of FAUs at its downside
EPS of NT$2 and the same 50x P/E to  an implied valuation of NT$108 for

CPO contribution, and Largan's total share price at NT$2,500.

Forecast returns
Forecast price appreciation -32.4%
Forecast dividend yield 1.1%
Forecast stock return -31.4%
Market return assumption 6.3%
Forecast excess return -37.6%
Company Description
Founded in 1987, Largan Precision (Largan) is one of the world's leading manufacturers of
lenses used in cameras and IT products. It specialises in plastic/hybrid lenses and, to a lesser
extent, glass lenses and modules. In 2022, 20MP+ camera lenses were 10-20% of Largan's

shipment mix, 10MP lenses were 50-60%, 8MP lenses were 0-10% and other lenses were
30-40%.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
Our price target is based on SOTP valuation on the CPO opportunity and traditional lens
business.
We think Largan's downside risks include: 1) ongoing pricing pressure from smartphone OEM
clients and increasingly intense competition among lens suppliers; and 2) a slower-than-
expected smartphone demand recovery in China, and 3) CPO commercial success below the

market's elevated expectations.
We believe the key upside risk is a faster-than-expected CPO qualification process at Largan.
In addition, if the company demonstrates exceptionally efficient and highly accurate
automation capabilities, it could support greater market share gains within the industry than
we currently anticipate.

KEY DEFINITIONS:Forecast Stock Return (FSR) is defined as expected percentage price appreciation plus gross dividend yield over
the next 12 months. In some cases, this yield may be based on accrued dividends. Market Return Assumption (MRA) is defined as
the one-year local market interest rate plus 5% (a proxy for, and not a forecast of, the equity risk premium). Under Review (UR)
Stocks may be flagged as UR by the analyst, indicating that the stock's price target and/or rating are subject to possible change in the

near term, usually in response to an event that may affect the investment case or valuation. Equity Price Targets have an investment
horizon of 12 months.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

### Page 3

Earnings revision: We factor in VPEC’s Jul-Aug revenues and raise our 2026-28E net 
incomes by 12% / 18% / 30%, mainly on higher revenues and GMs. 2026-28E revenues 
are revised up by 7% / 14% / 25%, mainly on higher contribution from optoelectronics 
business, driven by the strong end demand of AI data center optical networking, better 
InP substrate supply, and VPEC’s customers’ expansion and product expansion from CW 
lasers to EML, along with specification upgrade. Our 2026-28E GMs are revised up on 
rising contribution from optoelectronics business, which carries a higher margin than 
other product lines. We revise up 2027-28E opex ratios, mainly on higher R&D expense 
to collaborate with key customers on next generation optical networking products.

peers’ PEG&M of 0.4x (vs. 0.3x previously), we derive target PE multiple at 22.2x (vs.

### Page 8

VPEC focuses on III-V compound semiconductor EPI wafer for microelectronics and 
optoelectronics devices based on InP and GaA substrate, and expands to EPI wafer for 
SiPh transceivers CW laser for data center applications. We expect strong momentum in 
VPEC growth driven by the SiPh ramp up in 2026E, microelectronics clients’ increasing 
demand, and its accumulated technology advantages on EPI wafer. We note that VPEC is 
currently trading lower than our TP implied 69x 2027E forward P/E, which is attractive, 
in our view.  
Price Target Risks and Methodology - VPEC 
Valuation: We adopt a discounted P/E method and apply a 22.2x target P/E on VPEC’s 
2029E EPS, discounting back to 2027E at a COE of 10.5% (beta 1.7x, risk free rate 1.6% 
and market risk premium at 5.1%), to derive our 12m TP of NT$793. Our target P/E is 
based on 0.4x PEG&M ratio derived from the average level of peers in the photonics 
supply chain. 
Key risk: Slower-than-expected SiPh business ramp up; weaker-than-expected 
microelectronics demand from smartphones clients; IDM offers EPI wafer in-house.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.
Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.
Not Rated (NR). The investment rating, target price and earnings estimates (where relevant) are removed pursuant to Goldman Sachs policy when
Goldman Sachs is acting in an advisory capacity in a merger or in a strategic transaction involving this company, when there are legal, regulatory or
policy constraints due to Goldman Sachs’ involvement in a transaction, and in certain other circumstances. Early-Stage Biotech (ES). An investment

rating and a target price are not assigned pursuant to Goldman Sachs policy when this company has neither a drug, treatment or medical device that
has passed a Phase II clinical trial nor a license to distribute a post-Phase II drug, treatment or medical device.  Rating Suspended (RS). Goldman Sachs
Research has suspended the investment rating and price target for this stock, because there is not a sufficient fundamental basis for determining an
investment rating or target price. The previous investment rating and target price, if any, are no longer in effect for this stock and should not be relied

upon.  Coverage Suspended (CS). Goldman Sachs has suspended coverage of this company.  Not Covered (NC). Goldman Sachs does not cover this
company.

Yageo
Valuation
We set our target price at NT$1,450, based on 35x average 2027E-28E earnings. We value Yageo based on PE as we believe the
company’s earnings performance and industry dynamics would be the key focuses of the market. Our target multiple of 35x is
at the higher end of the trading range since 2022. During this period the company has gone through the cycles, successfully
integrated the acquired business to enhance its product offerings, and started to deliver stable margins and earnings.

Risks
Key downside risks to our target price and rating include: 1) slower-than-expected demand recovery; 2) intensified peer and
pricing competition; 3) execution risks associated with M&A integration and synergies; and 4) FX and interest rate fluctuations.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

E Ink Holdings Inc.  | Asia Pacific
August  Sales +20% MoM/+3%
Y oY
In this report, we focus on E Ink's monthly sales, which we
believe could be a catalyst for its share price.
Details:
• August sales were NT$3,446mn (+20% MoM/+3% YoY).
• QTD sales reached 63% of our 3Q26 revenue estimate of NT$9,985mn (-2%
QoQ/-4% YoY) and 62% of consensus at NT$10,213mn (flat QoQ/-2% YoY).
Our view:
• The solid rebound in the Aug monthly sales should alleviate the potential

risk of missing full-year guidance again (now at 10-15% YoY for 2026), in our
view, as  3Q26 revenue is tracking in line with our estimate for  a modest
decline QoQ.
• While the worst seems to be behind us and valuation is at a trough level of
12x 2027 P/E, we see no strong catalysts in the near term, despite our
positive long-term view on  e-paper demand across various applications.

Morgan Stanley Research 3
Valuation Methodology and Risks
E Ink Holdings Inc. (8069.TWO)
Base case, 12x 2027e P/E. We believe this valuation methodology best captures E Ink's earn-
ings growth momentum. We view our target multiple of 12x as fair, considering our estimated
17% earnings CAGR for 2025-28. The implied PEG of 0.7x is largely in line with its typical level
in 2022-25, when the stock was trading at an average P/E of 22x one-year forward consensus

EPS with a 37% earnings CAGR.
Risks to Upside
n Firmer-than-expected ESL orders
n Stronger demand for e-readers and e-notes
n Better macro conditions
n Milder competition
n Brisker inventory digestion
Risks to Downside
n Softer-than-expected ESL orders
n Slower demand for e-readers and e-notes
n Macro headwinds
n Intensified competition
n More protracted inventory digestion

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy ,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover . Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy , hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety , and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk -adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk -adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk -adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk -adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below .
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below .

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below .
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

MPI Corporation  | Asia Pacific
3Q Revenue Looks Better than
Feared; OW
MPI reported August sales of NT$2,048mn, +13% M/M and +70% Y/Y, above our
NT$1,876mn estimate –  mainly supported by price hikes, TPU, and other AI ASICs.
Assuming flat September revenue M/M, 3Q revenue would rise 13% Q/Q, above our
+7% estimate and the Street at +10% Q/Q.
Stay OW: As previously indicated, we expect 3Q revenue to exceed guidance given
CPC/VPC price hikes. With very strong micro bump demand and more migration to

2nm, we believe MPI needs to expand MEMS probe card capacity to at least 8–10mn
pins per month in 2027, positioning it to meet strong AI ASIC and GPU demand.
PCB material shortages remain a risk; however, MPI is diversifying suppliers and
seeking substitutes.
For CPO,  Insertion 2 qualification continues, with insertion 3 shipments on track to
start in 4Q26.
Investors were slightly frustrated by the conservative guidance, but still view MPI as
best positioned to benefit from increased chip probing demand over the longer term

Key Takeaways
The stock could come under pressure following management's conservative
guidance. However, we expect more updates to follow in coming quarters.
Strong micro bump demand and greater migration to 2nm lead the team to argue
that MEMS probe card capacity should expand to 8–10mn pins per month in
2027.
PCB material shortages remain an explicit risk. Mitigation centers on diversifying

suppliers/seeking substitutes to reduce reliance on constrained inputs.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
                        MPI Corporation (6223.TWO)                    
We derive our price target from a residual income model, as we believe this can best capture 
the long-term value. We assume a 9.78% cost of equity (risk-free rate 2.0% and risk premium 
6.0%), a payout ratio of 67%, a medium-term growth rate of 16.0%, and a terminal growth rate 
of 4.0%. Our price target implies 50x/25x 2027e/2028e P/E. We believe its valuation will con-
tinue to climb along with growing earnings.

n Stronger-than-expected AI demand
n Stronger-than-expected market share gain from peers
n Stronger-than-expected CPO development

Risks to Downside                    
n Slower-than-expected AI demand
n Slower-than-expected market share gain from peers
n Slower-than-expected CPO development

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

### Page 2

UMC (2303.TW)
11 September 2026 Citi Research
2
•2027 Pricing Looks More Favorable.  Management hopes to implement broader-based price increases across AI and
non-AI products to offset higher wafer, energy and other input costs and support new capacity investments. Pricing
should therefore turn more positive in 2027 if utilization remains high. Management noted that every 1% ASP
increase could improve gross margin by roughly 0.5–0.6ppt. However, margins are unlikely to revisit the >45%

COVID-era peak because depreciation is structurally higher following the current investment cycle.
•Intel 12nm cooperation: Capital-Light Path, contribution starting in late 2027 . The Intel collaboration is
strategically important because it gives UMC a capital-light path from its strong 22/28nm franchise into 12nm. Intel
provides existing Arizona manufacturing equipment and capacity, while UMC contributes technology conversion, RD
resources and its existing customer relationships. Initial customers are primarily Wi-Fi and communications-related,

with the first meaningful revenue expected around late 2027; broader contribution should build gradually thereafter
as customers validate the first wave. UMC expects to book the wafer revenue, with Intel manufacturing costs flowing
through UMC’s cost structure and economics broadly shared between the two parties. Expansion into smaller nodes
or advanced packaging with Intel remains possible, but management stressed that 12nm must first demonstrate

competitive yield, manufacturing performance and profitability.
•Silicon Photonics: Singapore Fab Becomes a Major AI Growth Platform.  The company is building dedicated 12-inch
capacity for silicon photonics in Singapore, with the new facility expected to be ready in roughly 14 months.  The
platform can encompass PICs and related components such as TIA, with management describing demand as
stronger than its available engineering resources.  UMC also has exposure to TFLN through an affiliate focused

initially on 6-inch technology, with potential migration to 8-inch manufacturing supported by UMC. Current
economics are attractive because photonics wafers can command multiples of conventional CMOS wafer pricing.
Longer term, UMC wants to combine silicon photonics, specialty materials and packaging capabilities into an
integrated platform.
•Advanced Packaging: Building an Open-Ecosystem Second Source. Advanced packaging represents UMC’s third
major structural growth driver. Rather than competing directly with TSMC’s fully integrated leading-edge ecosystem,

UMC aims to become a credible second-source, open-ecosystem provider. Its developing capabilities include
interposers, wafer-to-wafer and hybrid bonding, DTC, and related packaging processes, with silicon photonics
potentially integrated into the same broader solution. UMC is constructing two new advanced-packaging facilities in
Taiwan. Construction is expected to take more than two years, while existing facilities can provide limited capacity
during the interim.

•Specialty Strategy Provides Defense Against China Competition. UMC continues to differentiate itself through
specialty technologies rather than competing primarily on commodity mature-node capacity. Management
estimates 50–60%+ of revenue now comes from specialty processes, which provides meaningful insulation from
Chinese foundry pricing pressure. China’s semiconductor market is also becoming increasingly segmented into
“China-for-China” and “non-China-for-non-China” supply chains, limiting direct cross-border price competition.

UMC’s two China fabs remain highly utilized and represent roughly 10% of group capacity. Overall, UMC’s strategy is
shifting from being primarily a mature-node foundry toward a specialty and heterogeneous-integration platform
while improving utilization and pricing provide a stronger cyclical backdrop for 2027.

### Page 4

UMC (2303.TW)
11 September 2026 Citi Research
4
UMC
Valuation
Our target price for UMC of NT$125 is based on 15x 2027E EPS, which is higher than the midpoint of its 3-year forward PE. We
believe our target multiple is justified by our belief that UMC should benefit from the robust demand in 22/28nm, TSMC's
increasing order outflow opportunity, long-term opportunity in advanced packaging and photonics and that its niche
technology offerings should help it outperform its trailing-edge foundry peers. Our target price equates to 2026E/27E PB of

3.5x/3.2x.
Risks
Key downside risks to our target price include: 1) a longer-than-expected demand recovery; 2) more competition from peers in
28nm; 3) worse-than-expected developments from the company's China investment; 4) a significant decline in smartphone
end-demand; 5) poor contribution from Novatek's iPhone OLED business; and 6) fiercer ASP competition from Chinese peers.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

### Page 1

AI semi capex, in particular for compute chips, should outgrow
overall AI capex in 2028. We add cross-checks on ASICs after
AVGO’s guidance and the Qualcomm/AWS AI ASIC
announcement.
Will 2028 be another growth year for AI semis? Our US internet analyst Brian
Nowak published the Morgan Stanley AI Guidebook: Navigating the Age of
Inference, and suggests 12% CSP capex growth into 2028, raising investor questions
about AI semi growth momentum. Based on our latest supply chain checks, we

introduce preliminary 2028 CoWoS/CoPoS/EMIB-T capacity assumptions. In
aggregate, we still see 50% capacity growth in 2028 (see Exhibit 1), implying
flattish infrastructure (e.g., the shell and AI DC) spending and moderate memory
pricing in 2028.
Larger chip size and more applications (CPU, networking) to drive further
growth in 2028: All these packaging technologies are 2.5D (compute and memory
chips on side to side) for AI/HPC chips, including AI GPU, XPU, CPU, and networking.

•CoWoS (wafer based): We still see some CoW expansion at TSMC. For oS, TSMC
may need more partners than ASE. Our checks suggest both Amkor and Xintec
(non-covered) will diversify oS production in the US and Taiwan, respectively.
• CoPoS (panel-based): This is a panel-level process. After converting to 12-inch
CoWoS wafer equivalents, we assume 5–10kwpm in 2028. The initial target project
should be Nvidia’s Feynman Ultra GPU.
• EMIB-T (substrate-based): Although a substrate-based technology, we convert it

to 12-inch CoWoS wafer-equivalent capacity as well. One EMIB-T substrate can
generate 16 pcs of a 9x reticle-size chip, around 4x the chips a CoWoS wafer can
generate. Our checks indicate ~40k–45k wafer-equivalent EMIB-T capacity in 2028,
producing at least 2mn of Humufish (9x reticle) substrates. 40k–45k wafer
equivalents also imply 10-15% share in 2.5D packaging.
Read-across from recent ASIC guidance at Broadcom and the Qualcomm/AWS
partnership

•According to Joe Moore’s report: Broadcom Inc.: Earnings solid; raising FY 28 EPS,
more specific FY27 revenue guidance is now US$115 bn, and that could double again
in FY28. Our CoWoS supply-chain checks already see an AVGO AI lab customer
booking ~15k–30k CoWoS wafers for 2027.
•Qualcomm announced its ASIC partnership with AWS, a key Alchip ASIC customer.
However, the press release points to inference, different from Alchip’s Trainium4,
which covers both training and inference. We therefore see limited impact to Alchip.

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.

TSMC CoWoS and CoPoS
With continual booming demand in AI, we expect TSMC and its OSAT partners to expedite
capacity expansion plans for CoWoS. We expect TSMC's CoWoS capacity to further build
to 260kwpm in 2028 from 130/200kwpm in 2026/2027, with 2028's expansion mainly

focusing in Arizona's AP9/10 and potentially in AP7. TSMC is now conducting trial
production for CoPoS in Taoyuan. As long as the progress goes well, we expect TSMC to
start building its CoPoS capacity, with the location also likely to be in AP9/10 or AP7P3.
We expect the non-TSMC camp to expand CoWoS capacity to 110kwpm by 2028 year-end
from 80kwpm by end-2027. For ASE/SPIL, we expect FoCoS+CoWoS capacity to rise from
30kwpm at 2027-end to 50-60kwpm at 2028-end, with expansion focused on CoWoS-L

and CoWoS-R. For Amkor, we expect CoWoS capacity to increase from 30kwpm at 2027-
end to 55-60kwpm at 2028-end, also focused on CoWoS-L and CoWoS-R.
Exhibit 4: We expect TSMC's CoWoS capacity to further build
to 260kwpm in 2028, with OSAT (ASE/SPIL/Amkor) together
building to 110kwpm in the same year

How about  Intel EMIB capacity build?

### Page 4

M
Idea
4
For Intel, we see current average EMIB-M capacity being 110kwpm, which would slightly
decrease to 95kwpm in 2027 as it's converting some capacities toward EMIB-T. We see the
major customers of EMIB-M being Trainium3, upcoming Trainium 4 and its in-house server
CPU. Average EMIB-T capacity is around 5kwpm in 2026, which we expect to further scale
to 15-20kwpm in 2027 and 40-45kwpm in 2028. All of the capacity mentioned above is
converted for  12-inch wafer equivalent, which we believe would be much more easier to

communicate and compared with TSMC CoWoS capacity.
Although some small projects are ongoing, we still see MediaTek/Google Humufish
consuming most of the capacities during 2027-28. We maintain our view that Intel EMIB-T
could still  produce close to 3mn units of Humufish from late 2027-2028, by assuming 4-5
chips per wafer of EMIB-T.

in 2027 and 45kwpm in 2028; EMIB-M capacity is currently at

110kwpm, which would be down to 95kwpm due to conversion

2027 CoWoS allocation assumptions
Our recent industry checks suggest that the CoWoS capacity build of OSAT (both ASE and

Amkor) is slower than our initial expectation and customers' forecasts. Therefore, we now
see a slower ramp of AMD Venice CPU which should be the major customer of ASE and
Amkor CoWoS capacity.

CoWoS update (Exhibit 10):
• AMD's total 2027 CoWoS consumption increases 165% Y/Y to 345k:
° We expect AI GPU-related production to be all at TSMC CoWoS, while
MI455/450 would be the key focus for 2027, with minor production for MI500
series (chip code name Arcadia) in late 2027. Both MI455 and MI450 could
reach around 500-650k units respectively. We estimate total TSMC CoWoS-L
booking increases 200% Y/Y in 2027, to 210k.
° We see the non-TSMC camp, including ASE/SPIL and  Amkor, being mainly in

charge of Venice CPU, high-end PC CPU, and gaming GPU production.
Powertech was also planning for the production of Venice, but we now expect
it to focus on gaming GPU only, given the lower yield rate of panel level
packaging.
° However, given the slower production  ramp at OSAT,   we believe TSMC would

### Page 6

M
Idea
6
also need to support around 80k CoWoS-L for Venice production, partially
squeezing out the capacity of AI GPU build.
° We now expect Venice CPU's CoWoS booking to increase from 50k in 2026 to
210k in 2027, implying 4.4mn units of CPU production, lower than our initial
forecast of 5.6mn.
• Microsoft's Maia200 CoWoS booking increases from 1k in 2026e to 5k in 2027, we
estimate:
° We have been seeing upward revision demand of Microsoft Maia 200 into

2027; we believe the demand could reach around 5k of CoWoS in 2027,
suggesting around 100-150k chips.
• US AI Lab CoWoS booking could reach 15-30k in 2027:
° With the recent announcement of AI ASIC from one US AI lab, we expect its
CoWoS booking at TSMC to reach 15-30k in 2027, with support from
Broadcom.

Connecting Nvidia GPU shipments and rack shipments:
In Howard Kao’s rack shipment update, Greater China Technology Hardware: GB200 and
GB300 NVL72 Racks in August 2026 (9 Sep 2026), August racks recovered, implying
more Blackwell GPU consumption. His CY26 full-year rack forecast is now at 80-85K
racks. This is similar to our observation that KYEC continues final testing for meaningful
volumes of B-series GPUs in 3Q26.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

台新新光金 (2887 TT)  Buy

台新銀上修 2026 年 NIM 至 YoY+5~8bps 。壽險受惠台股上行，
1H26 底股票 OCI 未實現餘額大幅成長， 08/2026 回升至高於
07/2026 。研究部預估台新新光金 2026 年稅後純益為 899.40 億
元， YoY+140.95% ，稅後 EPS 為 3.39 元。股利政策方面， 2027

票股利仍留有彈性。預估仍將維持逾 4%現金殖利率。給予台新
新光金投資建議 Buy ，目標價 48 元 (2027 年 PBR1.49X) 。

2 群益投顧 
  台新新光金控 ESG ：  
台新新光金於 2023 年延續認真永續、綠色生活的永續主張，以實踐企業永
續經營為目標，包含公司治理、員工關懷、社會參與及公益推動。永續治理
方面，包含強化董事會及功能性委員會職能如台新金 2023 年度董事會舉辦
14 次會議；深化供應商永續發展如 2023 年以內部碳定價為永續主題，並連
續 5 年舉辦供應商大會。永續賦能方面，台新金提供高競爭力薪酬福利如連
續 10 年列入高薪 100 成分股；扶植包括弱勢群體之非營利組織如 2023 年
獲公益基金捐助社福團體 160 家。實績方面，包含 2023 年連續 6 年蟬聯
DSJI 世界指數與新興市場指數雙榜、 2024 年連續 4 年榮獲 MSCI ESG 評比
AA 領先等級 2 等。  
公司簡介：  
台新新光金以智慧好夥伴作為品牌定位，堅守認真態度，並以誠信、創新、
合作及承諾的核心價值滿足客戶需求，建立長久關係。期許在整合專業金融
人才及提供專業金融服務下，成為客戶最值得信賴的金控公司。 02/2002 台
新銀行合併大安銀行，成立台新金控； 08/2017 台新證券完成與大眾證券合
併； 08/2021 完成併購保德信人壽，並更名台新人壽； 05/2023 台新期貨開
業；04/2024 台新銀行福岡支行開業； 07/24/2025 台新金與新光金完成合併，
並更名為台新新光金。  
預估金控 2026 年稅後 EPS 3.39 元：  
台新新光金 1H26 累計稅後純益 437.97 億元， YoY+328.8% 。 1H26 底 ROA
0.97% 、 ROE 15.36% ，總資產達 9.39 兆元， YoY+177% 。  
資產品質方面， 1Q26 底 CAR 119.7% ，維持穩健資本。 1H26 淨值 YoY+236%
至 7,136 億元，換算每股淨值約 26.28 元。  
股利政策方面， 2026 年配發 2025 年度普通股每股現金股利 1 元、股票股利
0.1 元。公司表示， 2027 年股利政策仍將維持考量子公司獲利狀況、現金殖
利率及總股利率與同業比較、穩定成長且具可預期之股利等之基調。人壽盈
餘上繳尚待主管機關核准。 2027 年股利配發將優於 2026 年，並以現金股利
為主，且是否配發股票股利仍留有彈性。考量公司並未揭露金控加計 OCI 股
票資本利得之調整後獲利，故研究部以過去 5 年現金配發率 37~65% 中緣為
預期基礎，推估 2027 年有望配發現金股利 1.8 元，並以 09/01/2026 收盤價
換算，現金殖利率約 4.5% 。  
關於併購案產生之 6,000 多億元商譽 (付出價金扣除新光金被購買之市場價
值差異 )，公司表示商譽將於每年執行減損措施，不會做攤銷，亦不會由 PPA
沖銷，且以當前狀況仍未見需認列減損狀況。收購價格分攤 (PPA) 方面，大
部分來自合併新光人壽，將多數反映於新光人壽每個月損益，截至 2025 年
底約認列 30 億元 PPA ，預估 2026 年 PPA 攤銷約 80~100 億元。  
台新新光金 07/2026 自結稅後純益 89.4 億元， MoM-0.22% 、YoY+146.96% ，
主因人壽子公司 CSM 穩定攤銷及經常性投資收益同步挹注，加上現金股利
入帳較 06/2026 成長，帶動人壽 07/2026 獲利 MoM+18.72% ；01~07/2026 累
計稅後純益 527.8 億元， YoY+281.36% ，稅後 EPS 2.04 元。展望 2H26 ，併
購綜效仍將持續挹注業績表現，積極實現股票資本利得及台股上揚，推升人

目標。 01/01/2026 CSM 開帳餘額 2,607 億元， 1H26 釋出 95 億元 CSM 、新

推估 2026 年底 CSM 餘額可望達逾 3,300 億元， 2027 年約可釋出 195~235
億元 CSM 。

56.1% 。受惠於加權指數及 AI 類股強漲， 06/2026 底股債未實現餘額近 900
億元，其中股票未實現餘額約 810 億元。 07/2026 適時處分部位獲利入袋，
雖因台股下跌有所收斂 ， 惟08/2026 未實現餘額已高於 07/2026 。截 至06/2026
底淨值比約 9.98% ， YTD+74% ，顯著提升。  
投資績效方面， 1H26 避險前經常性收益率 4.19% ，較 2025 年提升 0.85% ，
主因合併後重新以市價評估資產 。 還原後則約 3.43%；1H26 避險成本 1.22%，
優於預期，主因傳統避險工具 (CS 、 NDF) 成本降低； 1H26 負債成本 2.3% ，
符合預期； 1H26 避險後投報率約 3.73% ，較 2025 年提升 1.07% ，若加計 OCI
股票處分損益 2.12% 後則為 5.85% ，較 2025 年提升 3.19% ，換算利差益分別
為 1.43% 、 3.55% 。  
截至 1H26 底外幣資產約 2.06 兆元，其中外幣保單佔 30.3% 、傳統避險 (CS、
NDF) 比率約 32.2% ，外價金餘額達 1,085 億元。  
新光人壽 07/2026 稅後純益 57.7 億元， MoM+18.72% 、 YoY+220.55% ，
01~07/2026 累計稅後純益 282.7 億元， YoY+1,470.55% 。展望 2H26 ， 1H26
底股票未實現餘額約 810 億元，雖 07/2026 因指數下跌而回落，惟 08/2026
未實現餘額已較 07/2026 高，明顯回升。故研究部維持財務結果正向看法。
保險服務結果方面， 1H26 新契約 CSM 約達成 2026 年目標之 50% ，符合先
前預期，故仍維持 2026 年新契約 CSM 逾 700 億元目標。研究部預估 2026
年底 CSM 餘額可望達逾 3,300 億元，以 6~7% 攤銷率為基礎，推估 2027 年
可望釋出 195~235 億元 CSM。預期 2H26 仍將以高保障型產品、長年期及健
康險保單等為核心產品主軸。另外，截至 1H26 底外價準備金餘額 1,085 億
元，預估匯率波動大致不影響損益。研究部預估新光人壽 2026 年稅後純益
為 417.98 億元， YoY+1,930.0% 。  
台新金、新光金子公司併購進程如預期 ：  
台新金及新光金之合併案，於 10/09/2024 雙方股東臨時會審議通過，
3/31/2025 獲金管會核准， 07/24/2025 台新金與新光金完成合併，並更名為
台新新光金。 11/24/2025 台新投信與新光投信完成合併。 01/01/2026 台新人
壽與新光人壽完成合併。 04/06/2026 台新證券與元富證券完成合併。台新銀
行與新光銀行已於 08/2026 初取得主管機關核准函，預計 01/01/2027 正式
合併，當前整併進度符合公司預期。  
投資建議：  
台新銀及新光銀均維持原放款、財管手收預期，且台新銀上修 2026 年 NIM
至 YoY+5~8bps 。壽險受惠台股上行， 1H26 底股票 OCI 未實現餘額大幅成
長， 08/2026 回升至高於 07/2026 。研究部預估台新新光金 2026 年稅後純益
為 899.40 億元， YoY+140.95% ，稅後 EPS 為 3.39 元。股利政策方面， 2027
年股利配發將優於 2026 年，並以現金股利為主，且是否配發股票股利仍留
有彈性。預估仍將維持逾 4%現金殖利率。給予台新新光金投資建議 Buy ，
目標價 48 元 (2027 年 PBR1.49X) 。

10 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」 ，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」 、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，首
次評等時潛在報酬率不同。 「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

3324.TT 雙鴻 
 水冷板重回 NVIDIA Vera Rubin 供應鏈

[財務資訊更新]

公司簡介 
雙鴻成立於 1998 年 8 月，主要生產散熱器、散熱片、散熱模組、散熱導管、風扇等，
應用於筆記型電腦 、 桌上型電腦 、 伺服器 、 投影機等領域 ， 主要終端客戶為Apple(AAPL 
US)、Dell(DELL US)、Samsung(005930 KS)、HP(HPQ US)、 宏碁(2353)、 華碩(2357)
等。 
投資評等/目標價 重點摘要 
買進 1,620 
【 前 次 投 資 建 議 】區 間 操 作，1,260 
【大盤指數/股價】47106 / 1340

➢ 2H26 新品逐步放量，營運動能增溫  
雙鴻 8 月營收 31.4 億元，MoM-16.6%，YoY+67.2%， 營收月減是受客
戶拉貨排程影響，以及 7 月基期較高所致。雙鴻 1H26 伺服器營收占比
已突破 70%(水冷營收占比已達 55%)，3Q26 為新舊產品交替期，整體
2H26 在 ASIC 與 GPU 伺服器的強勢帶動下，營運展望樂觀，公司維持
全年營收 YoY+70%的目標不變： 
1)隨著搭載新一代晶片的 AISC 伺服器問世，ASIC 散熱方案預期會在
4Q26~1Q27 明顯推升雙鴻業績 ， 且ASIC 客戶持續擴大水冷散熱導入 ，
此部分效益可挹注到 2027 年；2)雙鴻 3Q26 cold plate 出貨以 GB300
為大宗 ， 近期宣布其Vera Rubin cold plate， 已回到NVIDIA 推薦名單 ，
預期 4Q26 小量出貨；3)其他新品方面，DIMM cold plate 已於 2Q26
小量出貨。CPU 均熱片 2026 年營收占比為個位數，要到 2027~2028
年效益才會較明顯。 
➢ 2027 年泰國持續擴產 
雙鴻生產基地位於台灣、中國(廣州/昆山/重慶/合肥)、泰國，泰國廠為
近年擴產重點 ：1)泰國一期廠區已於 2025 年投產 ， 主要生產cold plate、
manifold、Rack CDU 等液冷散熱元件，目前產能為 cold plate 250k/
月、manifold 3k/ 月；2)泰國二期於 2026 年 9 月正式量產，主要生產
cold plate 、manifold，並預留電源水冷產能，若各產品線產能順利拉
升 ， 未來泰國各項產品產能可翻倍 ；3)泰國三期規劃在 4Q26 動工 、2H27
投產；4)泰國四期預計 2028 年動工。 
➢ 建議調升至買進，目標價 1,620 元  
雙鴻 2H26 營運展望正向，AI 伺服器的強勢增長，能彌補消費性電子市
況低迷帶來的不利影響。 3Q26 GB 300 散熱產品出貨持續增量，並且
cold plate 已重新回到 NVIDIA Vera Rubin 推薦名單，預期 4Q26 小量
出貨，而 ASIC 客戶之新品亦逐漸放量中，有利營運回溫。雙鴻歷史 PE
區間為 8X~36X， 考量新品可逐步帶動2H26~1H27 之業績 ， 且目前PE
評價偏低，建議逢低買進，目標價 1,620 元(2027F PE 20X)。

個股報告

2H26 新品逐步放量，營運動能增溫 
雙鴻 8 月營收 31.4 億元，MoM-16.6%，YoY+67.2%，營收月減是受客戶拉貨排程
影響，以及 7 月基期較高所致(7 月營收包含 6 月客戶盤點遞延拉貨的 3 億元)。雙鴻
1H26 伺服器營收占比已突破 70%(水冷營收占比已達 55%)，3Q26 為新舊產品交替
期，整體 2H26 在 ASIC 與 GPU 伺服器的強勢帶動下，營運展望樂觀，公司維持全年
營收 YoY+70%的目標不變： 
1)隨著搭載新一代晶片的 AISC 伺服器問世 ，ASIC 散熱方案預期會在 4Q26~1Q27 明
顯推升雙鴻業績，且 ASIC 客戶持續擴大水冷散熱導入，此部分效益可挹注到 2027
年。雙鴻估計 2026 年底 ASIC 客戶將占單月營收達 40%，2027 年 ASIC 營收占比將
擴增至 50%；2)雙鴻 3Q26 cold plate 出貨以 GB300 為大宗 ， 近期宣布其Vera Rubin 
cold plate ，已回到 NVIDIA 推薦名單，預期 4Q26 小量出貨，如此一來公司能提供
涵蓋 cold plate 、Rack Manifold 、Inner Manifold 的完整方案；3)其他新品方面，
DIMM cold plate 已於 2Q26 小量出貨。CPU 均熱片(Heat Spreader)2026 年營收占
比為個位數，要到 2027 年~2028 年效益才會較明顯。 
投研部預期，在新舊平台完成交接，新品順利放量的情境下，雙鴻伺服器營收占比將
上升至 80%以上，受惠新品效應與水冷的導入，可帶動毛利率回升，投研部預估雙鴻
3Q26 營收 101.9 億元，QoQ+17.2%，YoY+71.1%，毛利率 29.8%，EPS 達 15.40
元。 
圖 1：雙鴻產品組合 1Q25~2Q26

資料來源：雙鴻 
2027 年泰國持續擴產   
雙鴻生產基地位於台灣、中國(廣州/昆山/重慶/合肥)、泰國， 泰國廠為近年擴產重點 。
各廠區規劃如下 ：1)泰國一期廠區已於 2025 年投產 ， 主要生產cold plate、manifold、
Rack CDU 等液冷散熱元件，目前產能為 cold plate 250k/月、manifold 3k/月；2)泰
國二期於 2026 年 9 月正式量產，主要生產 cold plate、manifold，並預留電源水冷
產能，若各產品線產能順利拉升，未來泰國各項產品產能可翻倍； 3)泰國三期規劃在
4Q26 動工、2H27 投產；4)泰國四期預計 2028 年動工；5)中國廠區負責生產筆電、
高階顯示卡 、 伺服器散熱相關產品 ， 整體上 ， 以氣冷模組較多 ， 也有少量的cold plate、
manifold；6)台灣主力生產 CDU (Cooling Distribution Unit)，並負責高階與高精度
液冷產品的研發和樣品試產。

建議調升至買進，目標價 1,620 元 
雙鴻 2H26 營運展望正向，AI 伺服器的強勢增長，能彌補消費性電子市況低迷帶來的
不利影響 。3Q26 GB 300 散熱產品出貨持續增量 ， 並且cold plate 已重新回到 NVIDIA 
Vera Rubin 推薦名單，預期 4Q26 小量出貨，而 ASIC 客戶之新品亦逐漸放量中，有
利營運回溫。雙鴻歷史 PE 區間為 8X~36X，考量新品可逐步帶動 2H26~1H27 之業
績，且目前 PE 評價偏低，建議逢低買進，目標價 1,620 元(2027F PE 20X)。
